```
+def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
+    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
+        return _serialize_cluster_item(item)
 
+    serialized = _serialize_issue_item_base(item)
+    _add_workflow_state(serialized, item)

     return serialized

+def _normalize_issue_detail(raw_detail: object) -> dict:
+    """Normalize issue detail payload to a dict with expected defaults."""

+def _render_cluster_action_steps(action_steps: list[dict | str]) -> None:
+    """Render the first few action steps for a cluster item."""
+    if not action_steps:
+        return
+    show_count = min(3, len(action_steps))
+    for i, step in enumerate(action_steps[:show_count], 1):
+        marker = "[x]" if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("done") else "[ ]"
+        print(colorize(f"    {i}. {marker} {_step_display_text(step)}", "dim"))
+    remaining = len(action_steps) - show_count
+    if remaining > 0:
+        print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more — drill in to view all", "dim"))
+
+

 _HEX8_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{8}$")
 
 
+def _all_known_issue_ids(state: dict, plan: dict | None) -> list[str]:
+    """Collect issue IDs from state, queue order, and cluster membership."""
+    all_ids: list[str] = list(state.get("issues", {}).keys())
+    if plan is None:
+        return all_ids
+
+    seen_ids: set[str] = set(all_ids)
+    for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []):
+        if fid in seen_ids:
+            continue
+        seen_ids.add(fid)
+        all_ids.append(fid)
+    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
+        for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
+            if fid in seen_ids:
+                continue
+            seen_ids.add(fid)
+            all_ids.append(fid)
+    return all_ids
+
+
+def _print_suggestions(pattern: str, suggestions: list[str], *, tip: str | None = None) -> None:
+    """Print a standard suggestion block for an unmatched pattern."""
+    if not suggestions:
+        return
+    print(colorize(f"  No match for: {pattern!r}", "yellow"))
+    print(colorize("  Did you mean:", "dim"))
+    for match in suggestions[:3]:
+        print(colorize(f"    {match}", "dim"))
+    if tip:
+        print(colorize(f"  Tip: {tip}", "dim"))
+
+
+def _hash_suffix_suggestions(all_ids: list[str], suffix: str) -> list[str]:
+    """Return ID suggestions for an 8-char hash suffix pattern."""
+    return [fid for fid in all_ids if fid.endswith(f"::{suffix}") or fid == suffix]
+
+
+def _slug_suggestions(all_ids: list[str], *, last_seg: str, slug: str) -> list[str]:
+    """Return ID suggestions for a slug/segment pattern."""
+    suggestions: list[str] = []
+    for fid in all_ids:
+        if f"::{last_seg}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{last_seg}"):
+            suggestions.append(fid)
+            continue
+        if slug and (f"::{slug}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{slug}")):
+            suggestions.append(fid)
+    return suggestions
+
+
```

```
+def _cluster_tags(cluster: dict) -> str:
+    """Build display tags for cluster metadata."""
+    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
+    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
+    auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
+    tags: list[str] = []
+    if auto:
+        tags.append("auto")
+    if desc:
+        tags.append("desc")
+    else:
+        tags.append("no desc")
+    if steps:
+        tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
+    elif not auto:
+        tags.append("no steps")
+    return f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
+
+
+def _print_active_clusters(active_clusters: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
+    """Render currently active clusters with tags and descriptions."""
+    if not active_clusters:
+        return
+    print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
+    for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
+        count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
+        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
+        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
+        print(f"    {name}: {count} items{_cluster_tags(cluster)}{desc_str}")
+
+
+def _unclustered_issue_ids(clusters: dict, open_issues: dict) -> list[str]:
+    """Return issue IDs not currently assigned to any cluster."""
+    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
+    for cluster in clusters.values():
+        all_clustered.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
+    return [fid for fid in open_issues if fid not in all_clustered]
+

+def _print_unclustered_issues(unclustered: list[str], open_issues: dict) -> None:

+def _print_completed_clusters(completed: list[dict]) -> None:

     return failures
 
 
+def _validate_observe_stage(stages: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
+    if "observe" not in stages:
+        return False, "Observe stage not recorded."
+    report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
+    if len(report) < 100:
+        return False, f"Observe report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
+    cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
+    issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
+    if issue_count <= 0:
+        return True, ""
+    min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10))
+    if len(cited) >= min_citations:
+        return True, ""
+    return (
+        False,
+        f"Observe report cites only {len(cited)} issue(s) "
+        f"(need {min_citations}+). Reference specific issue "
+        f"hashes to prove you read them.",
+    )
+
+
+def _validate_reflect_stage(stages: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
+    if "reflect" not in stages:
+        return False, "Reflect stage not recorded."
+    report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
+    if len(report) < 100:
+        return False, f"Reflect report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
+    return True, ""
+
+
+def _organize_warnings(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
+    """Collect advisory warnings for organize stage quality."""
+    warnings: list[str] = []
+    overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
+    if overlaps:
+        warnings.append(f"{len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) share files without dependencies")
+    scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
+    if scattered:
+        names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _ in scattered)
+        warnings.append(f"Theme-grouped clusters (5+ dirs): {names}")
+    high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
+    if high_ratio:
+        names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _, _ in high_ratio)
+        warnings.append(f"1:1 step-to-issue ratio: {names}")
+    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
+    orphaned = [
+        n for n, c in clusters.items()
+        if not c.get("auto") and not c.get("issue_ids") and c.get("action_steps")
+    ]
+    if orphaned:
+        warnings.append(f"Orphaned clusters (steps, no issues): {', '.join(orphaned)}")
+    return warnings
+
+
+def _validate_organize_stage(plan: dict, state: dict, stages: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:
+    if "organize" not in stages:
+        return False, "Organize stage not recorded."
+    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
+    if not manual:
+        return False, "No manual clusters with issues exist."
+    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
+    if gaps:
+        names = ", ".join(n for n, _ in gaps)
+        return False, f"Unenriched clusters: {names}"
+    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
+    if unclustered:
+        return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
+    warnings = _organize_warnings(plan)
+    if warnings:
+        return True, "Advisory: " + "; ".join(warnings)
+    return True, ""
+
+
+def _validate_enrich_quality_stage(
+    *,
+    stage: str,
+    plan: dict,
+    repo_root: Path,
+    stages: dict,
+) -> tuple[bool, str]:
+    if stage not in stages:
+        return False, f"{stage.capitalize()} stage not recorded."
+    if stage == "sense-check":
+        report = stages["sense-check"].get("report", "")
+        if len(report) < 100:
+            return False, f"Sense-check report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
+    failures = run_enrich_quality_checks(plan, repo_root, phase_label=stage)
+    if failures:
+        return False, failures[0].message
+    return True, ""
+
+

+def _missing_or_unconfirmed_required_stages(stages: dict) -> str | None:
+    """Return the first missing/unconfirmed required stage message, if any."""
+    for required in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"):
+        if required not in stages:
+            return f"Stage {required} not recorded."
+        if not stages[required].get("confirmed_at"):
+            return f"Stage {required} not confirmed."
+    return None
+
+
+def _self_dependent_cluster_name(clusters: dict) -> str | None:
+    """Return cluster name when it depends on itself, else None."""
+    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
+        deps = cluster.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
+        if name in deps:
+            return name
+    return None
+
+
+def _all_trivial_manual_clusters(clusters: dict) -> list[str]:
+    """Return sorted manual cluster names where all steps are trivial."""
+    names: list[str] = []
+    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
+        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
+            continue
+        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
+        if steps and all(
+            isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("effort") == "trivial"
+            for step in steps
+        ):
+            names.append(name)
+    return sorted(names)
+
+
```

---

## Analysis of 8 Refactoring Commits

### Overall Pattern

Commits 1-7 extract inline code into single-use helper functions. Commit 8 then **reverts most of what commits 3, 4, and 5 did** to those same files, putting the code back inline. This means the branch is churning -- extracting code in one commit and un-extracting it in another. The net effect of commits 3+8, 4+8, and partially 5+8 is largely zero for the files they share. I'll focus on what remains after commit 8 (the final state).

---

### Commit 1: `a3d773f` -- reduce next/plan render path cyclomatic complexity

**Verdict: Overzealous. Several extractions add indirection without benefit.**

**Specific problems:**

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/next/output.py` -- `_add_workflow_state()` and `_add_plan_metadata()` are void functions that mutate a dict passed in. The original code was 15 lines of straightforward if-checks inline in `serialize_item()`. Extracting them into two separate mutating functions makes the data flow harder to follow (you now need to track that `serialized` is being mutated by side-effect across function calls). `_add_plan_metadata` uses a loop over keys that obscures the `plan_skipped` special case that follows.

2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/next/render.py` -- `_normalize_issue_detail()`, `_render_issue_detail_fields()`, `_render_issue_code_snippet()`, and `_render_plan_item_context()` are all called exactly once from `_render_issue_detail()`. The original was ~30 lines of linear code; now it's 4 function calls plus 4 function definitions (~65 lines of code total). Each helper is trivial (3-8 lines of logic). `_render_special_item()` wraps two checks that were perfectly readable as 4 lines inline.

3. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/next/render_support.py` -- `_render_cluster_action_steps()`, `_render_cluster_members()`, and `_render_cluster_primary_action()` are each called once from `render_cluster_item()`. The original `render_cluster_item` was already a well-scoped function. Breaking it into sub-functions just adds indirection.

4. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py` -- `_all_known_issue_ids()`, `_print_suggestions()`, `_hash_suffix_suggestions()`, and `_slug_suggestions()` are all single-use extractions from `_suggest_close_matches()`. The original was ~30 lines of clear procedural code. Now you have to read 4 function signatures to understand the same logic.

---

### Commit 2: `0b92cbf` -- split plan command complexity hotspots

**Verdict: Overzealous in some places, reasonable in others.**

**Specific problems:**

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py` -- `_plan_queue_line()`, `_skip_kind_counts()`, `_print_cluster_summary()`, `_print_commit_tracking()` are all single-use extractions from `_cmd_plan_show()`. `_skip_kind_counts()` returns a 4-tuple that must be unpacked -- the original 6 lines of inline code were more readable. `_plan_queue_line()` wraps a try/except that was fine inline. `_print_commit_tracking()` hides a config load behind a function name -- the original inline code with `_cfg` made it clear config was loaded once.

2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/plan/commit_log_handlers.py` -- `_resolve_commit_identity()` returns a tuple `(sha, branch)` where the caller then checks `if not sha: return`. The original inline code was more straightforward. `_select_commit_issue_ids()` uses `[]` vs `None` as a sentinel value, which is a subtle API contract (the caller checks `if issue_ids == []: return`). This is worse than the original which just had `if not uncommitted` and `if not issue_ids`.

3. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py` -- `_resolve_effective_cluster()`, `_build_queue_items()`, `_compute_visible_new_ids()`, `_render_cluster_banners()`, `_render_queue_rows()` are all single-use. The original `cmd_plan_queue()` was a linear function. Now it dispatches to 5 helpers, each with their own keyword args. `_build_queue_items()` returns a `tuple[list[dict], dict]` which makes the caller less readable.

---

### Commit 3: `c55b861` -- simplify triage display and stage validation flow

**Verdict: Mixed. Display extractions are borderline reasonable; stage_validation lambda-dispatch is worse than if/elif.**

**Specific problems (that survive commit 8):**

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/display.py` -- `_cluster_tags()`, `_print_active_clusters()`, `_unclustered_issue_ids()`, `_print_unclustered_issues()`, `_print_completed_clusters()`, `_print_resolved_issue_deltas()`, `_print_recurring_or_first_triage()` -- all single-use. The functions that were extracted from `print_progress()` and `print_reflect_dashboard()` are borderline. The originals were medium-length but clear. The extraction is not harmful but adds no significant value either.

Note: The `stage_validation.py` changes from this commit (lambda dispatch table) are **reverted** by commit 8, which puts the if/elif chain back. So this was wasted churn.

---

### Commit 4: `7dd2811` -- simplify triage completion and review import flows

**Verdict: Significantly overzealous in `helpers.py`. Mostly reverted by commit 8.**

The `stage_completion_commands.py` and `triage_handlers.py` changes are reverted by commit 8. What survives is in `review/importing/`:

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/cmd.py` -- `_resolve_import_payload()`, `_build_working_state()`, `_apply_assessment_policy()`, `_raise_on_partial_skip()`, `_append_assessment_import_audit()`, `_persist_import_state()` -- six single-use extractions from `do_import()`. The original was a sequential pipeline that read top-to-bottom. Now you have to hop between 6 helper functions. `_build_working_state()` is 4 lines. `_persist_import_state()` is 4 lines. These don't justify their own functions.

2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/helpers.py` -- This file gets the worst treatment. The original `_normalize_import_payload_shape()` was a single function with inline validation for each field. Commit 4 explodes it into `_normalize_issues()`, `_normalize_optional_mapping()`, `_normalize_reviewed_files()`, `_normalize_policy()`. Then commit 8 **reverts this back** to the inline version. Similarly, `_parse_and_validate_import()` was split into `_load_raw_import_payload()`, `_normalize_raw_import_payload()`, `_validate_assessment_flag_combo()`, `_apply_assessment_policy_checked()`, `_manual_override_attest_errors()`, `_validate_assessment_feedback_requirements()`, `_schema_validation_errors()` -- and commit 8 reverts most of this back inline.

**Net result after commit 8**: `helpers.py` goes through massive churn (split then unsplit) but the `print_assessment_policy_notice()` function stays split into 5 per-mode print functions (`_print_trusted_notice`, `_print_trusted_internal_notice`, etc.) dispatched via a lambda dict. The original if/elif chain was clearer.

---

### Commit 5: `9404e4f` -- reduce review merge and prompt complexity

**Verdict: Overzealous in merge.py. The prompt_sections changes are reasonable.**

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/review/merge.py` -- `_resolve_similarity()`, `_open_holistic_review_items()`, `_group_duplicate_issue_concepts()`, `_apply_merge_group()`, `_print_merge_group_summary()` are all single-use extractions from `do_merge()`. The original was a ~80-line function. `_resolve_similarity()` is a 4-line try/except/clamp. `_open_holistic_review_items()` is a one-liner list comprehension. `_apply_merge_group()` returns a `tuple[str, list[str]]` -- but it also mutates `group` items in place as a side effect, which the return type doesn't communicate. This is actually *worse* than the original where the mutation was visible inline.

   **After commit 8**: `merge.py` changes from commit 5 are **reverted**, so this was pure churn.

2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py` -- `_concern_signal_lines()` and `_iter_valid_concern_signals()` are reasonable extractions. The loop body was non-trivial. This is fine.

---

### Commit 6: `73b5d34` -- split scan reconcile pipeline without short-circuiting

**Verdict: Actively harmful. Introduces a bug pattern and makes code harder to understand.**

**Specific problems:**

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` -- `_run_sync_steps()` wraps a list of lambdas that each return bool. The original was a simple sequence of `if step(): dirty = True` calls. The lambda list adds indirection without benefit. Worse, `_sync_post_scan_without_policy()` and `_sync_post_scan_with_policy()` wrap groups of these calls. The final call site has:
   ```python
   dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state)
   dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(...) or dirty
   ```
   The `or dirty` placement is correct but fragile -- if someone changes the order to `dirty = dirty or _sync_post_scan_with_policy(...)`, Python's short-circuit evaluation would skip the second call when `dirty` is already `True`. The original code had no such footgun.

2. Also in this commit: `_sync_communicate_score_and_log()` now creates a `ScoreSnapshot` object inline with field-by-field copy from `state_mod.score_snapshot()`. This is new logic introduced alongside the refactoring that should be in a separate commit.

---

### Commit 7: `c5a7171` -- switch review import APIs to config-first signatures

**Verdict: Worst offender. Adds `**legacy_kwargs` to public APIs, creating a strictly worse interface.**

**Specific problems:**

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/cmd.py` -- `do_import()` and `do_validate_import()` now accept `**legacy_kwargs` alongside `import_config: ReviewImportConfig | None`. The `_legacy_import_config()` function validates `legacy_kwargs` at runtime by checking allowed keys. This replaces explicit typed keyword arguments with an untyped `**kwargs` bag -- losing IDE autocompletion, type checking, and clear function signatures. The CLAUDE.md explicitly says "No backward compatibility for import paths or APIs" -- so this compatibility shim contradicts the project's own policy.

2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/helpers.py` -- Same pattern: `load_import_issues_data()` now takes `**legacy_kwargs` instead of named parameters. A frozen `LegacyImportLoadArgs` dataclass is introduced solely to validate these kwargs at runtime. This is ~60 lines of validation/coercion code (`_legacy_bool`, `_legacy_text`, `_coerce_legacy_import_load_kwargs`, `LegacyImportLoadArgs`) that replaces zero lines -- it's a backward-compat shim for an internal tool that doesn't need backward compat.

---

### Commit 8: `23021a4` -- move plan operations to option objects

**Verdict: This commit is mostly a revert of commits 3, 4, and 5. The operation object changes are fine but should have been the only content.**

This commit does two things:
1. **Reverts** the helper extractions from commits 3 (stage_validation, triage_handlers, stage_completion_commands), 4 (stage_completion_commands, triage_handlers), and 5 (merge.py) back to inline code.
2. Makes changes to `engine/_plan/` operation modules (commit_tracking, operations_meta, operations_skip).

The reverts confirm that those extractions were overzealous -- the author recognized this and undid them. But this creates a messy commit history where 3 commits of work are silently reverted in a later commit.

---

## Summary of Concrete Problems to Address

**Patterns that should be reverted/cleaned up (still in the tree after commit 8):**

1. **Commit 7's `**legacy_kwargs` pattern** in `cmd.py` and `helpers.py` -- violates the project's explicit no-backward-compat policy, replaces typed signatures with untyped kwargs bags, adds ~100 lines of runtime validation that shouldn't exist. Files:
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/cmd.py`
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/helpers.py`

2. **Commit 6's lambda-list sync pipeline** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` -- `_run_sync_steps()` with lambda lists is more fragile and less readable than the original sequential `if step(): dirty = True` pattern.

3. **Commit 1's trivial single-use extractions** in `output.py`, `render.py`, `render_support.py`, and `cluster_handlers.py` -- add indirection without reducing complexity. Particularly bad: void-mutating functions `_add_workflow_state()` and `_add_plan_metadata()`.

4. **Commit 2's trivial single-use extractions** in `cmd.py`, `commit_log_handlers.py`, and `queue_render.py` -- the tuple-return patterns (`_skip_kind_counts` returning a 4-tuple, `_select_commit_issue_ids` using `[]` as sentinel) are worse than inline code.

5. **Surviving helper.py splits from commit 4** -- the `print_assessment_policy_notice` dispatch via lambda dict in `helpers.py` is more complex than the original if/elif chain.

```
    52→    allow_partial: bool,
    53→    override_enabled: bool,
    54→    override_attest: str | None,
    55→) -> None:
    56→    """Fail fast on conflicting import flags to keep behavior explicit."""
    57→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
    58→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    59→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
    60→        )
    61→    if attested_external and allow_partial:

    75→def imported_assessment_keys(issues_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
    77→    raw_assessments = issues_data["assessments"]
    78→    keys: set[str] = set()
    79→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
    80→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
    81→        if normalized:
    82→            keys.add(normalized)
    83→    return keys

    86→def mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
    87→    state: dict,
    88→    *,
    89→    assessment_keys: set[str],
    90→) -> int:
    91→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
    92→    if not assessment_keys:
    93→        return 0
    94→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    95→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
    96→        return 0
    97→
    98→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
    99→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   100→    marked = 0
   101→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   102→        payload = store.get(key)
   103→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   104→            continue
   105→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
   106→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   107→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   108→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   109→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   110→        marked += 1
   111→    return marked
   112→
   113→
   114→def clear_provisional_override_flags(
   115→    state: dict,
   116→    *,
   117→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   118→) -> int:
   119→    """Clear provisional override flags when trusted assessments replace them."""
   120→    if not assessment_keys:
   121→        return 0
   122→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   123→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   124→        return 0
   125→
   126→    cleared = 0
   127→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   128→        payload = store.get(key)
   129→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   130→            continue
   131→        if payload.pop("provisional_override", None) is not None:
   132→            cleared += 1
   133→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   134→        if payload.get("source") == "manual_override":
   135→            payload["source"] = "holistic"
   136→    return cleared
   137→
```

```
    58→    legacy_kwargs: dict[str, object],
    59→) -> ReviewImportConfig:
    60→    """Resolve config object from modern argument or legacy keyword args."""
    61→    allowed_keys = {
    62→        "config",
    63→        "allow_partial",
    64→        "trusted_assessment_source",
    65→        "trusted_assessment_label",
    66→        "attested_external",
    67→        "manual_override",
    68→        "manual_attest",
    69→    }
    70→    unknown = sorted(set(legacy_kwargs) - allowed_keys)
    71→    if unknown:
    72→        joined = ", ".join(unknown)
    73→        raise TypeError(f"Unexpected keyword argument(s): {joined}")
    74→    if import_config is not None and legacy_kwargs:
    75→        raise TypeError("Pass either import_config=... or legacy keyword args, not both")
    76→    if import_config is not None:
    77→        return import_config
    78→    config_obj = legacy_kwargs.get("config")
    79→    config_dict = config_obj if isinstance(config_obj, dict) else None
    80→    return ReviewImportConfig(
    81→        config=config_dict,
    82→        allow_partial=_optional_bool(legacy_kwargs.get("allow_partial"), default=False),
    83→        trusted_assessment_source=_optional_bool(
    84→            legacy_kwargs.get("trusted_assessment_source"),
    85→            default=False,
    86→        ),
    87→        trusted_assessment_label=_optional_text(
    88→            legacy_kwargs.get("trusted_assessment_label")
    89→        ),
    90→        attested_external=_optional_bool(
    91→            legacy_kwargs.get("attested_external"),
    92→            default=False,
    93→        ),
    94→        manual_override=_optional_bool(
    95→            legacy_kwargs.get("manual_override"),
    96→            default=False,
    97→        ),
    98→        manual_attest=_optional_text(legacy_kwargs.get("manual_attest")),
    99→    )
   100→
   101→
   102→def _resolve_import_payload(
   103→    import_file,
   104→    *,
   105→    lang_name: str,
   106→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig,
   107→) -> tuple[dict, bool, str | None]:
   108→    """Validate import flags and load payload with policy checks."""
   109→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
   110→        manual_override=import_config.manual_override,
   111→        manual_attest=import_config.manual_attest,
   112→    )
   113→    try:
   114→        validate_import_flag_combos(
   115→            attested_external=import_config.attested_external,
   116→            allow_partial=import_config.allow_partial,
   117→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
   118→            override_attest=override_attest,
   119→        )
   120→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
   121→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
   122→
   123→    try:
   124→        issues_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_issues_data(
   125→            import_file,
   126→            config=build_import_load_config(
   127→                lang_name=lang_name,
   128→                import_config=import_config,
   129→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
   130→                override_attest=override_attest,
   131→            ),
   132→        )
   133→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   134→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
   135→            exc.errors,
   136→            import_file=str(import_file),
   137→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   138→        )
   139→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
   140→
   141→    return issues_data, override_enabled, override_attest
   142→
   143→
   144→def _build_working_state(state: dict, state_file) -> dict:
   145→    """Return state snapshot used for import mutation/dry-run rendering."""
   146→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
   147→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
   148→        return copy.deepcopy(state_mod.load_state(state_path))
   149→    return copy.deepcopy(state)
   150→
   151→
   152→def _apply_assessment_policy(
   153→    *,
   154→    working_state: dict,
   155→    issues_data: dict,
   156→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   157→) -> int:
   158→    """Apply provisional/clear flags based on assessment policy mode."""
   159→    assessment_keys = imported_assessment_keys(issues_data)
   160→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   161→        return mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   162→            working_state,
   163→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   164→        )
   165→    if assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   166→        clear_provisional_override_flags(
   167→            working_state,
   168→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   169→        )
   170→    return 0
   171→
   172→
   173→def _raise_on_partial_skip(diff: dict, *, allow_partial: bool) -> None:
   174→    """Refuse import when payload skips issues and partial imports are disabled."""
   175→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) <= 0 or allow_partial:
   176→        return
   177→    details_lines: list[str] = []
   178→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   179→        reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   180→        details_lines.append(
   181→            f"  #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}"
   182→        )
   183→    msg = "import produced skipped issue(s); refusing partial import."
   184→    if details_lines:
   185→        msg += "\n" + "\n".join(details_lines)
   186→    msg += "\nFix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override."
   187→    raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
   188→
   189→
   190→def _append_assessment_import_audit(
   191→    *,
   192→    working_state: dict,
   193→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   194→    provisional_count: int,
   195→    override_attest: str | None,
   196→    import_file,
   197→) -> None:
   198→    """Record audit metadata for assessment-bearing import payloads."""
   199→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   200→        return
   201→    audit = working_state.setdefault("assessment_import_audit", [])
   202→    audit.append(
   203→        {
   204→            "timestamp": state_mod.utc_now(),
   205→            "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   206→            "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   207→            "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   208→            "override_used": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   209→            "attested_external": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "attested_external"),
   210→            "provisional": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   211→            "provisional_count": int(provisional_count),
   212→            "attest": (override_attest or "").strip(),
   213→            "import_file": str(import_file),
   214→        }
   215→    )
   216→
   217→
   218→def _persist_import_state(
   219→    *,
   220→    state: dict,
   221→    working_state: dict,
   222→    state_file,
   223→    diff: dict,
   224→    assessment_mode: str,
   225→) -> None:
   226→    """Persist imported state and synchronize the work plan."""
   227→    state.clear()
   228→    state.update(working_state)
   229→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   230→    sync_plan_after_import(state, diff, assessment_mode)
   231→
   232→
   233→def do_import(
   234→    import_file,
   235→    state,
   236→    lang,
   237→    state_file,
   238→    *,
   239→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig | None = None,
   240→    dry_run: bool = False,
   241→    **legacy_kwargs,
   242→) -> None:
   243→    """Import mode: ingest agent-produced issues."""
   244→    resolved_import_config = _legacy_import_config(
   245→        import_config=import_config,
   246→        legacy_kwargs=legacy_kwargs,
   247→    )
   248→    issues_data, _override_enabled, override_attest = _resolve_import_payload(
   249→        import_file,
   250→        lang_name=lang.name,
   251→        import_config=resolved_import_config,
   252→    )
   253→
   254→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel = (
   255→        import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(issues_data)
   256→    )
   257→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   258→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   259→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   260→    )
   261→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   262→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   263→        import_file=str(import_file),
   264→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   265→    )
   266→
   267→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   268→    working_state = _build_working_state(state, state_file)
   269→
   270→    diff = review_mod.import_holistic_issues(issues_data, working_state, lang.name)
   271→    label = "Holistic review"
   272→    provisional_count = _apply_assessment_policy(
   273→        working_state=working_state,
   274→        issues_data=issues_data,
   275→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   276→    )
   277→    _raise_on_partial_skip(diff, allow_partial=resolved_import_config.allow_partial)
   278→    _append_assessment_import_audit(
   279→        working_state=working_state,
   280→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   281→        provisional_count=provisional_count,
   282→        override_attest=override_attest,
   283→        import_file=import_file,
   284→    )
   285→
   286→    if not dry_run:
   287→        _persist_import_state(
   288→            state=state,
   289→            working_state=working_state,
   290→            state_file=state_file,
   291→            diff=diff,
   292→            assessment_mode=assessment_policy.mode,
   293→        )
   294→
   295→    display_state = state if not dry_run else working_state
   296→    print_import_results(
   297→        state=display_state,
   298→        lang_name=lang.name,
   299→        config=resolved_import_config.config,
   300→        diff=diff,
   301→        prev=prev,
   302→        label=label,
   303→        provisional_count=provisional_count,
   304→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   305→        scorecard_subjective_at_target_fn=_SCORECARD_SUBJECTIVE_AT_TARGET,
   306→    )
```

```
   210→
   211→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   212→    """Prepare or import subjective code review issues."""
   213→    _enable_live_review_output()
   214→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   215→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   216→    state = runtime.state
   217→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   218→    _require_lang(lang)
   219→
   220→    opts = ReviewOptions.from_args(args)
   221→    mode_flags = _mode_flags(opts)
   222→    _validate_mode_selection(
   223→        opts,
   224→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   225→    )
   226→
   227→    # For default prepare and --run-batches modes, require a completed scan
   228→    # so the command doesn't hang building context from an empty/missing state.
   229→    if _is_default_prepare_mode(opts, mode_flags) or opts.run_batches:
   230→        if not require_completed_scan(state):
   231→            return
   232→
   233→    _run_review_mode(
   234→        args,
   235→        opts=opts,
   236→        runtime=runtime,
   237→        state=state,
   238→        lang=lang,
   239→        state_file=state_file,
   240→    )
   241→
```

Fix the overzealous `_run_sync_steps` lambda-list pattern in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py`.

Currently lines ~390-443 have:
```python
def _run_sync_steps(step_fns: list[Callable[[], bool]]) -> bool:
    dirty = False
    for step_fn in step_fns:
        if step_fn():
            dirty = True
    return dirty

def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(...) -> bool:
    return _run_sync_steps([
        lambda: _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan),
        lambda: _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, state),
    ])

def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(...) -> bool:
    return _run_sync_steps([
        lambda: _sync_stale_and_log(...),
        lambda: _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(...),
        lambda: _sync_communicate_score_and_log(...),
        lambda: _sync_create_plan_and_log(...),
        lambda: _sync_triage_and_log(...),
        lambda: _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(...),
        lambda: _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(...),
    ])
```

This wraps straightforward sequential calls in lambdas for no benefit. Replace with the original simple pattern:

```python
def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(*, plan, state) -> bool:
    dirty = False
    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
        dirty = True
    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, state):
        dirty = True
    return dirty

def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(*, plan, state, target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed) -> bool:
    dirty = False
    if _sync_stale_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy, cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed):
        dirty = True
    if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(plan, state, target_strict=target_strict, policy=policy, cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed):
        dirty = True
    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
        dirty = True
    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
        dirty = True
    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
        dirty = True
    if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
        dirty = True
    if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
        dirty = True
    return dirty
```

## 1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next/output.py`

Inline `_add_workflow_state()` and `_add_plan_metadata()` back into `serialize_item()`. These are void functions that mutate a dict passed in — the original inline code was clearer. The result should look like:

```python
def serialize_item(item):
    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
        return _serialize_cluster_item(item)

    serialized = _serialize_issue_item_base(item)
    
    # Workflow state
    blocked_by = item.get("blocked_by")
    if blocked_by:
        serialized["blocked_by"] = blocked_by
    if item.get("is_blocked"):
        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    explain = item.get("explain")
    if explain is not None:
        serialized["explain"] = explain
    
    # Plan metadata
    for key in ("queue_position", "plan_description", "plan_note", "plan_cluster"):
        value = item.get(key)
        if value:
            serialized[key] = value
    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
        skip_reason = item.get("plan_skip_reason")
        if skip_reason:
            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
    
    return serialized
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""

    10→

    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "kind": member.get("kind", "issue"),
    19→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    20→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    21→        "file": member.get("file"),
    22→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    23→        "status": member.get("status"),
    24→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def _serialize_cluster_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    29→    """Serialize cluster queue meta-items with sampled members."""
    30→    members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    31→    serialized_members = [
    32→        _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    33→        for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    34→    ]
    35→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    36→    serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    37→        "id": item.get("id"),
    38→        "kind": "cluster",
    39→        "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    40→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    41→        "member_count": member_count,
    42→        "members": serialized_members,
    43→        "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    44→        "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    45→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    46→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    47→    }
    48→    if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    49→        serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    50→        serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    51→
    52→    autofix_hint = item.get("autofix_hint")
    53→    if autofix_hint:
    54→        serialized_cluster["autofix_hint"] = autofix_hint
    55→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
    56→    if action_steps:
    57→        serialized_cluster["action_steps"] = action_steps
    58→    return serialized_cluster
    59→
    60→

    65→        "kind": item.get("kind", "issue"),
    66→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    67→        "detector": item.get("detector"),

    76→def _add_workflow_state(
    77→    serialized: dict[str, Any],
    78→    item: Mapping[str, Any],
    79→) -> None:
    80→    """Attach workflow and explanation metadata."""
    81→    blocked_by = item.get("blocked_by")
    82→    if blocked_by:
    83→        serialized["blocked_by"] = blocked_by
    84→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    85→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    86→    explain = item.get("explain")
    87→    if explain is not None:
    88→        serialized["explain"] = explain

    93→    item: Mapping[str, Any],
    94→) -> None:

    97→        value = item.get(key)
    98→        if value:
    99→            serialized[key] = value
   100→
   101→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   102→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
   103→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
   104→        skip_reason = item.get("plan_skip_reason")
   105→        if skip_reason:
   106→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   107→
   108→
   109→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   110→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
   111→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   112→        return _serialize_cluster_item(item)
   113→
   114→    serialized = _serialize_issue_item_base(item)
   115→    _add_workflow_state(serialized, item)
   116→    _add_plan_metadata(serialized, item)
   117→    return serialized
   118→
   119→
   120→def build_query_payload(
   121→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
   122→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   123→    *,
   124→    command: str,
   125→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   126→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   127→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   128→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
   129→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   130→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
   131→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   132→    }
   133→
   134→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   135→        "command": command,
   136→        "items": serialized,
   137→        "queue": queue_section,
   138→        "narrative": narrative,
   139→        "agent_notes": [
   140→            "Do NOT use `desloppify plan skip` unless the user explicitly asks you to skip an item.",
   141→            "If you cannot fix an item, report it and move to the next one.",
   142→            "For cluster items: if the autofix_hint finds 0 results, drill into individual issues with the primary_command.",
   143→        ],
   144→    }
   145→
   146→    if plan and (
   147→        plan.get("queue_order")
   148→        or plan.get("skipped")
   149→        or plan.get("clusters")
   150→    ):
   151→        clusters_summary = []
   152→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   153→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   154→            clusters_summary.append({
   155→                "name": name,
   156→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   157→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   158→            })
   159→        payload["plan"] = {
   160→            "active": True,
   161→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   162→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   163→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   164→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   165→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   166→        }
   167→
   168→    return payload
   169→
   170→
   171→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   172→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   173→    lines = [
   174→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   175→        "",
   176→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   177→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   178→    ]
   179→    for item in items:
   180→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   181→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   182→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   183→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   184→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   185→    lines.append("")
   186→    return "\n".join(lines)
   187→
   188→
   189→def write_output_file(
   190→    output_file: str,
   191→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   192→    item_count: int,
   193→    *,
   194→    safe_write_text_fn,
   195→    colorize_fn,
   196→) -> bool:
   197→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   198→    try:
   199→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   200→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   201→    except OSError as exc:
   202→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   203→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   204→        return False
   205→    return True
   206→
   207→
   208→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   209→    output_format: str,
   210→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   211→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   212→) -> bool:
   213→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   214→    renderers = {
   215→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   216→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   217→    }
   218→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   219→    if renderer is None:
   220→        return False
   221→    renderer()
   222→    return True
   223→
   224→
   225→__all__ = [
   226→    "build_query_payload",
   227→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   228→    "render_markdown",
   229→    "serialize_item",
   230→    "write_output_file",
   231→]
   232→
```

```
    42→def _render_subjective_dimension(item: dict, *, explain: bool) -> None:
    43→    """Render a subjective dimension re-review item."""
    44→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
    45→    subjective_score = float(
    46→        detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    47→    )
    48→    print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
    49→    print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}%")
    50→    print(
    51→        colorize(
    52→            f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
    53→            "cyan",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    print(colorize(
    57→        "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
    58→        "dim",
    59→    ))
    60→    print_user_message(
    61→        "Hey — this is a subjective review item. Run"
    62→        " `desloppify review --run-batches --dry-run`"
    63→        " to generate prompt files (one per batch)."
    64→        " Launch one subagent per prompt, all in"
    65→        " parallel. Then import with `--import-run"
    66→        " <run-dir> --scan-after-import`. Batches are"
    67→        " pre-defined — do NOT regroup them yourself."
    68→    )
    69→    if explain:
    70→        reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
    71→            "policy",
    72→            "subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.",
    73→        )
    74→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
    75→
    76→
    77→def _render_plan_item_context(
    78→    item: dict,
    79→    *,
    80→    single_item: bool,
    81→    header_showed_plan: bool,
    82→) -> None:
    83→    """Render plan metadata context attached to an item."""
    84→    plan_description = item.get("plan_description")
    85→    if plan_description:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  → {plan_description}", "cyan"))
    87→
    88→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
    89→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
    90→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
    91→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
    92→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
    93→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
    94→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
    95→
    96→        steps = plan_cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    97→        if steps and single_item and not header_showed_plan:
    98→            print(colorize("\n  Steps:", "dim"))
    99→            for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   100→                print(colorize(f"    {i}. {_step_text(step)}", "dim"))
   101→
   102→    plan_note = item.get("plan_note")
   103→    if plan_note:
   104→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {plan_note}", "dim"))
   105→
   106→
   107→def _normalize_issue_detail(raw_detail: object) -> dict:
   108→    """Normalize issue detail payload to a dict with expected defaults."""
   109→    if isinstance(raw_detail, str):
   110→        raw_detail = {"suggestion": raw_detail}
   111→    detail = dict(raw_detail) if isinstance(raw_detail, dict) else {}
   112→    detail.setdefault("lines", [])
   113→    detail.setdefault("line", None)
   114→    detail.setdefault("category", None)
   115→    detail.setdefault("importers", None)
   116→    detail.setdefault("count", 0)
   117→    return detail
   118→
   119→
   120→def _render_issue_detail_fields(detail: dict) -> None:
   121→    """Render optional detail fields for the issue card."""
   122→    lines = detail.get("lines")
   123→    if lines:
   124→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in lines[:8])}")
   125→    category = detail.get("category")
   126→    if category:
   127→        print(f"  Category: {category}")
   128→    importers = detail.get("importers")
   129→    if importers is not None:
   130→        print(f"  Active importers: {importers}")
   131→    suggestion = detail.get("suggestion")
   132→    if suggestion:
   133→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {suggestion}", "dim"))
   134→
   135→
   136→def _render_issue_code_snippet(item: dict, detail: dict) -> None:
   137→    """Render contextual code snippet around the primary issue line."""
   138→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   139→    file_path = item.get("file")
   140→    if not target_line or file_path in (".", ""):
   141→        return
   142→    snippet = read_code_snippet(file_path, target_line)
   143→    if not snippet:
   144→        return
   145→    print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   146→    print(snippet)
   147→
   148→
   149→def _render_issue_detail(
   150→    item: dict, *, single_item: bool = False, header_showed_plan: bool = False,
   151→) -> dict:
   152→    """Render plan overrides, file info, and detail fields. Returns parsed detail dict."""
   153→    _render_plan_item_context(
   154→        item,
   155→        single_item=single_item,
   156→        header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,
   157→    )
   158→
   159→    file_val = item.get("file", "")
   160→    if file_val and file_val != ".":
   161→        print(f"  File: {file_val}")
   162→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   163→
   164→    detail = _normalize_issue_detail(item.get("detail", {}))
   165→    _render_issue_detail_fields(detail)
   166→    _render_issue_code_snippet(item, detail)
   167→
   168→    return detail
   169→
   170→
   171→def _render_score_impact(
   172→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict | None,
   173→) -> None:
   174→    _render_score_impact_impl(
   175→        item,
   176→        dim_scores,
   177→        potentials,
   178→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   179→        log_fn=log,
   180→        compute_health_breakdown_fn=compute_health_breakdown,
   181→        compute_score_impact_fn=compute_score_impact,
   182→        get_dimension_for_detector_fn=get_dimension_for_detector,
   183→    )
   184→
   185→
   186→_KIND_RENDERERS = {
   187→    "cluster": _render_cluster_item,
   188→    "workflow_stage": _render_workflow_stage,
   189→    "workflow_action": _render_workflow_action,
   190→}
   191→
   192→
   193→def _render_item_type(item: dict) -> None:
   194→    detector = item.get("detector")
   195→    if detector == "review":
   196→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   197→        return
   198→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   199→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   200→
   201→
   202→def _render_auto_fix_batch_hint(item: dict, issues_scoped: dict) -> None:
   203→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   204→    if not is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   205→        return
   206→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   207→    similar_count = sum(
   208→        1
   209→        for issue in issues_scoped.values()
   210→        if issue.get("detector") == detector_name and issue["status"] == "open"
   211→    )
   212→    if similar_count <= 1:
   213→        return
   214→    print(
   215→        colorize(
   216→            f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar issues. "
   217→            f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   218→            "cyan",
   219→        )
   220→    )
   221→
   222→
   223→def _render_item_explain(
   224→    item: dict, detail: dict, confidence: str, dim_scores: dict,
   225→) -> None:
   226→    _render_item_explain_impl(
   227→        item,
   228→        detail,
   229→        confidence,
   230→        dim_scores,
   231→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   232→        get_dimension_for_detector_fn=get_dimension_for_detector,
   233→    )
   234→
   235→
   236→def _render_special_item(
   237→    item: dict,
   238→    *,
   239→    explain: bool,
   240→) -> bool:
   241→    """Render kind-specific items that bypass the standard issue card."""
   242→    kind = item.get("kind")
   243→    kind_renderer = _KIND_RENDERERS.get(kind)
   244→    if kind_renderer is not None:
   245→        kind_renderer(item)
   246→        return True
   247→    if item.get("kind", "issue") == "subjective_dimension":
   248→        _render_subjective_dimension(item, explain=explain)
   249→        return True
   250→    return False
   251→
   252→
   253→def _render_standard_issue_header(item: dict, confidence: str) -> None:
   254→    """Render the shared issue header before detail sections."""
   255→    print(colorize(f"  ({confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   256→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   257→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   258→    _render_item_type(item)
   259→
   260→
   261→def _render_item(
   262→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, issues_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   263→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   264→    single_item: bool = False,
   265→    header_showed_plan: bool = False,
   266→) -> None:
   267→    if _render_special_item(item, explain=explain):
   268→        return
   269→
   270→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   271→    _render_standard_issue_header(item, confidence)
   272→
   273→    detail = _render_issue_detail(
   274→        item, single_item=single_item, header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,
   275→    )
   276→    _render_score_impact(item, dim_scores, potentials)
   277→    _render_auto_fix_batch_hint(item, issues_scoped)
   278→    if explain:
   279→        _render_item_explain(item, detail, confidence, dim_scores)
   280→
   281→
   282→def _item_label(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> str:
   283→    queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   284→    if queue_pos and total > 1:
   285→        return f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   286→    if total > 1:
   287→        return f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}]"
   288→    pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   289→    return f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   290→
   291→
   292→def render_terminal_items(
   293→    items: list[dict],
   294→    dim_scores: dict,
   295→    issues_scoped: dict,
   296→    *,
   297→    group: str,
   298→    explain: bool,
   299→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   300→    plan: dict | None = None,
   301→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   302→) -> None:
   303→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster or cluster filter
   304→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   305→    header_showed_plan = False
   306→    if effective_cluster and plan:
   307→        cluster_name = effective_cluster
   308→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   309→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   310→        total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   311→        desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   312→        print(colorize(f"\n  ┌─ Cluster: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining) ─┐", "cyan"))
   313→        if desc:
   314→            print(colorize(f"  │ {desc}", "cyan"))
   315→        steps = cluster_data.get("action_steps") or []
   316→        if steps:
   317→            print(colorize("  │", "cyan"))
   318→            print(colorize("  │ Action plan:", "cyan"))
   319→            for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   320→                print(colorize(f"  │   {i}. {_step_text(step)}", "cyan"))
   321→        print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 60 + "┘", "cyan"))
   322→        print(colorize("  Back to full queue: desloppify next", "dim"))
   323→        if steps:
   324→            print(colorize(f"  Mark step done: desloppify plan cluster update {cluster_name} --done-step N", "dim"))
   325→        header_showed_plan = True
   326→
   327→    if group != "item":
   328→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   329→        return
   330→
   331→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   332→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   333→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   334→    )
   335→
   336→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   337→        if idx > 0:
   338→            print()
   339→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   340→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   341→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   342→            continue
   343→        label = _item_label(item, idx, len(items))
   344→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   345→        _render_item(
   346→            item, dim_scores, issues_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials,
   347→            single_item=len(items) == 1,
   348→            header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,
   349→        )
   350→
   351→
   352→__all__ = [
   353→    "render_terminal_items",
   354→]
   355→
```

```
    53→def effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:

   127→def _step_display_text(step: str | dict) -> str:
   128→    """Extract display text from an action step (string or dict with title)."""
   129→    if isinstance(step, dict):

   134→def _render_cluster_action_steps(action_steps: list[dict | str]) -> None:

   141→        print(colorize(f"    {i}. {marker} {_step_display_text(step)}", "dim"))
   142→    remaining = len(action_steps) - show_count
   143→    if remaining > 0:

   166→def render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:

   170→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   171→    is_optional = bool(item.get("cluster_optional"))
   172→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(cluster_name, action_type)
   173→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
   174→    done_count = sum(1 for s in action_steps if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("done"))
   175→    step_badge = f" [{done_count}/{len(action_steps)} steps done]" if action_steps else ""
   176→    optional_tag = " — optional" if is_optional else ""
   177→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} issues{step_badge}{optional_tag})", "bold"))
   178→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   179→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   180→
   181→    _render_cluster_action_steps(action_steps)
   182→
   183→    dep_order = item.get("dependency_order")
   184→    if dep_order is not None and dep_order <= 2:
   185→        print(colorize("  Priority: complete before other clusters", "cyan"))
   186→
   187→    _render_cluster_members(item.get("members", []))

   198→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   199→    del explain

   201→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   202→    # When the only item is the "run scan" workflow action, show "Queue cleared"
   203→    # instead of a misleading "1 items" count.
   204→    if (
   205→        total == 1
   206→        and len(items) == 1
   207→        and items[0].get("id") == "workflow::run-scan"
   208→    ):
   209→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared (1 workflow step)", "bold"))
   210→    else:
   211→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   212→        if total > 5:
   213→            print(colorize("  (Skip items only when explicitly requested.)", "dim"))

   216→def show_empty_queue(
   217→    queue: dict,
   218→    strict: float | None,
   219→    *,
   220→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   221→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   222→) -> bool:

   224→    if queue.get("items"):

   226→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   227→        delta = format_plan_delta(strict, plan_start_strict)
   228→        delta_str = f" ({delta})" if delta else ""
   229→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))

   231→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   232→            "cyan",

   234→        print(colorize(
   235→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   236→            "dim",
   237→        ))
   238→        return True
   239→
   240→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   241→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   242→    return True

   245→def render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   246→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   247→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   248→    tag = effort_tag(item)
   249→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   250→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   251→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) and (plan_cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   252→        tag_str += " [plan]"
   253→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   254→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   255→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   256→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   257→
```

```
    30→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{8}$")
    31→
    32→
    33→def _all_known_issue_ids(state: dict, plan: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Collect issue IDs from state, queue order, and cluster membership."""
    35→    all_ids: list[str] = list(state.get("issues", {}).keys())
    36→    if plan is None:
    37→        return all_ids
    38→
    39→    seen_ids: set[str] = set(all_ids)
    40→    for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    41→        if fid in seen_ids:
    42→            continue
    43→        seen_ids.add(fid)
    44→        all_ids.append(fid)
    45→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    46→        for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
    47→            if fid in seen_ids:
    48→                continue
    49→            seen_ids.add(fid)
    50→            all_ids.append(fid)
    51→    return all_ids
    52→
    53→
    54→def _print_suggestions(pattern: str, suggestions: list[str], *, tip: str | None = None) -> None:
    55→    """Print a standard suggestion block for an unmatched pattern."""
    56→    if not suggestions:
    57→        return
    58→    print(colorize(f"  No match for: {pattern!r}", "yellow"))
    59→    print(colorize("  Did you mean:", "dim"))
    60→    for match in suggestions[:3]:
    61→        print(colorize(f"    {match}", "dim"))
    62→    if tip:
    63→        print(colorize(f"  Tip: {tip}", "dim"))
    64→
    65→
    66→def _hash_suffix_suggestions(all_ids: list[str], suffix: str) -> list[str]:
    67→    """Return ID suggestions for an 8-char hash suffix pattern."""
    68→    return [fid for fid in all_ids if fid.endswith(f"::{suffix}") or fid == suffix]
    69→
    70→
    71→def _slug_suggestions(all_ids: list[str], *, last_seg: str, slug: str) -> list[str]:
    72→    """Return ID suggestions for a slug/segment pattern."""
    73→    suggestions: list[str] = []
    74→    for fid in all_ids:
    75→        if f"::{last_seg}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{last_seg}"):
    76→            suggestions.append(fid)
    77→            continue
    78→        if slug and (f"::{slug}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{slug}")):
    79→            suggestions.append(fid)
    80→    return suggestions
    81→
    82→
    83→def _suggest_close_matches(state: dict, plan: dict | None, patterns: list[str]) -> None:
    84→    """Print fuzzy match suggestions for patterns that resolved to zero issues."""
    85→    all_ids = _all_known_issue_ids(state, plan)
    86→
    87→    for pattern in patterns:
    88→        segments = pattern.split("::")
    89→        last_seg = segments[-1]
    90→
    91→        if _HEX8_RE.match(last_seg):
    92→            suggestions = _hash_suffix_suggestions(all_ids, last_seg)
    93→            _print_suggestions(
    94→                pattern,
    95→                suggestions,
    96→                tip=f"match by hash suffix alone: {last_seg}",
    97→            )
    98→            continue
    99→
   100→        slug = segments[-2] if len(segments) >= 2 else ""
   101→        _print_suggestions(
   102→            pattern,
   103→            _slug_suggestions(all_ids, last_seg=last_seg, slug=slug),
   104→        )
   105→
   106→
   107→def _print_pattern_hints() -> None:
   108→    """Print valid pattern format hints after a no-match error."""
   109→    print(colorize("  Valid patterns:", "dim"))
   110→    print(colorize("    f41b3eb7              (8-char hash suffix from dashboard)", "dim"))
   111→    print(colorize("    review::path::name    (ID prefix)", "dim"))
   112→    print(colorize("    review                (all issues from detector)", "dim"))
   113→    print(colorize("    src/foo.py            (all issues in file)", "dim"))
   114→    print(colorize("    timing_attack         (issue name — last ::segment of ID)", "dim"))
   115→    print(colorize("    review::*naming*      (glob pattern)", "dim"))
   116→    print(colorize("    my-cluster            (cluster name — expands to members)", "dim"))
   117→
   118→
   119→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   120→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   121→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   125→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   126→    dry_run: bool = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
   127→
   128→    plan = load_plan()
   129→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   130→    if not issue_ids:
   131→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   132→        _print_pattern_hints()
   133→        _suggest_close_matches(state, plan, patterns)
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if dry_run:
   137→        print(colorize(f"  [dry-run] Would add {len(issue_ids)} item(s) to {cluster_name}:", "cyan"))
   138→        for fid in issue_ids:
   139→            print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   140→        return
   141→
   142→    try:
   143→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, issue_ids)
   144→    except ValueError as ex:
   145→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    member_set = set(issue_ids)
   149→    for other_name, other_cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   150→        if other_name == cluster_name or other_cluster.get("auto"):
   151→            continue
   152→        other_ids = set(other_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   153→        if not other_ids:
   154→            continue
   155→        overlap = member_set & other_ids
   156→        if len(overlap) > len(other_ids) * 0.5:
   157→            percent = int(len(overlap) / len(other_ids) * 100)
   158→            print(
   159→                colorize(
   160→                    f"  Warning: {len(overlap)} issue(s) also in cluster '{other_name}' "
   161→                    f"({len(overlap)}/{len(other_ids)} = {percent}% overlap).",
   162→                    "yellow",
   163→                )
   164→            )
   165→
   166→    append_log_entry(plan, "cluster_add", issue_ids=issue_ids, cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user")
   167→    save_plan(plan)
   168→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
   169→
   170→
   171→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   172→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   173→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   177→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   178→    dry_run: bool = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
   179→
   180→    plan = load_plan()
   181→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   182→    if not issue_ids:
   183→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   184→        _print_pattern_hints()
   185→        _suggest_close_matches(state, plan, patterns)
   186→        return
   187→
   188→    if dry_run:
   189→        print(colorize(f"  [dry-run] Would remove {len(issue_ids)} item(s) from {cluster_name}:", "cyan"))
   190→        for fid in issue_ids:
   191→            print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    try:
   195→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, issue_ids)
   196→    except ValueError as ex:
   197→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   198→        return
   199→
   200→    append_log_entry(plan, "cluster_remove", issue_ids=issue_ids, cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user")
   201→    save_plan(plan)
   202→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
   203→
   204→
   205→def _cmd_cluster_update(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   206→    """Update cluster description, steps, and/or priority."""
   207→    _cmd_cluster_update_impl(args)
   208→
   209→
   210→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   211→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   212→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   213→    dispatch = {
   214→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   215→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   216→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   217→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   218→        "reorder": _cmd_cluster_reorder,
   219→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   220→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   221→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   222→        "merge": _cmd_cluster_merge,
   223→        "export": _cmd_cluster_export,
   224→        "import": _cmd_cluster_import,
   225→    }
   226→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   227→    if handler is None:
   228→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   229→        return
   230→    handler(args)
   231→
   232→
   233→__all__ = ["_cmd_cluster_add", "_cmd_cluster_remove", "_cmd_cluster_update", "cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
```

```json
  "new_string": "def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Build a serializable output dict from a queue item.\"\"\"\n    if item.get(\"kind\") == \"cluster\":\n        return _serialize_cluster_item(item)\n\n    serialized = _serialize_issue_item_base(item)\n\n    # Workflow state\n    blocked_by = item.get(\"blocked_by\")\n    if blocked_by:\n        serialized[\"blocked_by\"] = blocked_by\n    if item.get(\"is_blocked\"):\n        serialized[\"is_blocked\"] = True\n    explain = item.get(\"explain\")\n    if explain is not None:\n        serialized[\"explain\"] = explain\n\n    # Plan metadata\n    for key in (\"queue_position\", \"plan_description\", \"plan_note\", \"plan_cluster\"):\n        value = item.get(key)\n        if value:\n            serialized[key] = value\n    if item.get(\"plan_skipped\"):\n        serialized[\"plan_skipped\"] = True\n        serialized[\"plan_skip_kind\"] = item.get(\"plan_skip_kind\", \"temporary\")\n        skip_reason = item.get(\"plan_skip_reason\")\n        if skip_reason:\n            serialized[\"plan_skip_reason\"] = skip_reason\n\n    return serialized"
```

```json
  "old_string": "def _render_special_item(\n    item: dict,\n    *,\n    explain: bool,\n) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Render kind-specific items that bypass the standard issue card.\"\"\"\n    kind = item.get(\"kind\")\n    kind_renderer = _KIND_RENDERERS.get(kind)\n    if kind_renderer is not None:\n        kind_renderer(item)\n        return True\n    if item.get(\"kind\", \"issue\") == \"subjective_dimension\":\n        _render_subjective_dimension(item, explain=explain)\n        return True\n    return False\n\n\ndef _render_standard_issue_header(item: dict, confidence: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Render the shared issue header before detail sections.\"\"\"\n    print(colorize(f\"  ({confidence} confidence)\", \"bold\"))\n    print(colorize(\"  \" + \"─\" * 60, \"dim\"))\n    print(f\"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}\")\n    _render_item_type(item)\n\n\ndef _render_item(\n    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, issues_scoped: dict, explain: bool,\n    potentials: dict | None = None,\n    single_item: bool = False,\n    header_showed_plan: bool = False,\n) -> None:\n    if _render_special_item(item, explain=explain):\n        return\n\n    confidence = item.get(\"confidence\", \"medium\")\n    _render_standard_issue_header(item, confidence)\n\n    detail = _render_issue_detail(\n        item, single_item=single_item, header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,\n    )\n    _render_score_impact(item, dim_scores, potentials)\n    _render_auto_fix_batch_hint(item, issues_scoped)\n    if explain:\n        _render_item_explain(item, detail, confidence, dim_scores)",
  "new_string": "def _render_item(\n    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, issues_scoped: dict, explain: bool,\n    potentials: dict | None = None,\n    single_item: bool = False,\n    header_showed_plan: bool = False,\n) -> None:\n    # Kind-specific items that bypass the standard issue card\n    kind = item.get(\"kind\")\n    kind_renderer = _KIND_RENDERERS.get(kind)\n    if kind_renderer is not None:\n        kind_renderer(item)\n        return\n    if item.get(\"kind\", \"issue\") == \"subjective_dimension\":\n        _render_subjective_dimension(item, explain=explain)\n        return\n\n    # Standard issue header\n    confidence = item.get(\"confidence\", \"medium\")\n    print(colorize(f\"  ({confidence} confidence)\", \"bold\"))\n    print(colorize(\"  \" + \"─\" * 60, \"dim\"))\n    print(f\"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}\")\n    _render_item_type(item)\n\n    detail = _render_issue_detail(\n        item, single_item=single_item, header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,\n    )\n    _render_score_impact(item, dim_scores, potentials)\n    _render_auto_fix_batch_hint(item, issues_scoped)\n    if explain:\n        _render_item_explain(item, detail, confidence, dim_scores)"
```
